reports/phase4_evaluation.md:86:## Semantic support and provenance
reports/phase4_evaluation.md:88:Citation-ID validity and exact source/excerpt provenance are structural checks. They do not prove that a claim is semantically entailed. The review sheet therefore keeps `semantic_support_verdict=pending_human_review` (or lacks an explicit reviewer identity) and does not turn valid IDs or retrieval scores into a quality pass.
